Rows are\n// distributed in contiguous blocks over the MPI ranks; rank r owns rows\n// [rowStart(r), rowStart(r) + rowCount(r)).\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nstruct RowDist {\n    size_t n = 0;\n    int size = 1;\n    int rank = 0;\n\n    size_t rowStart(const int r) const noexcept { return (n * static_cast<size_t>(r)) / size; }\n    size_t rowCount(const int r) const noexcept { return rowStart(r + 1) - rowStart(r); }\n    size_t localStart() const noexcept { return rowStart(rank); }\n    size_t localCount() const noexcept { return rowCount(rank); }\n\n    // Owner rank of global row i.\n    int owner(const size_t i) const noexcept {\n        // Initial guess, then fix up (block sizes differ by at most one).\n        int r = static_cast<int>((i * static_cast<size_t>(size)) / n);\n        if (r >= size) r = size - 1;\n        while (r > 0 && rowStart(r) > i) --r;\n        while (r + 1 < size && rowStart(r + 1) <= i) ++r;\n        return r;\n    }\n};\n\n// Every rank replays the identical rand_r stream of the original program\n// (deterministic, seed 42) and keeps only the rows it owns. This costs O(n^2)\n// per rank, is outside the timed region, and needs no communication.\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const RowDist& rd,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    const size_t numNodes = rd.n;\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    const size_t first = rd.localStart() * numNodes;\n    const size_t last = first + rd.localCount() * numNodes;\n\n    // Skip the random values belonging to rows owned by lower ranks.\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < first; ++i) {\n        (void)rand_r(&seed);\n    }\n    for (size_t i = first; i < last; ++i) {\n        dist[i - first] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    const size_t r0 = rd.localStart();\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < rd.localCount(); ++i) {\n        dist[i * numNodes + (r0 + i)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Original initialization results in path[i*n + j] == j for all i, j\n// (the last write in the original loop wins and yields exactly this).\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const RowDist& rd) {\n    const size_t numNodes = rd.n;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < rd.localCount(); ++i) {\n        unsigned int* row = path.data() + i * numNodes;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n            row[j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Update local rows with intermediate node k, whose (already updated) row is\n// in rowK. Rows in [skipBegin, skipEnd) (local indices) are left untouched.\nstatic inline void relaxRows(unsigned int* __restrict dist, unsigned int* __restrict path,\n                             const unsigned int* __restrict rowK, const size_t numNodes,\n                             const size_t k, const size_t lBegin, const size_t lEnd) {\n    const unsigned int kk = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);\n    for (size_t li = lBegin; li < lEnd; ++li) {\n        unsigned int* __restrict d = dist + li * numNodes;\n        unsigned int* __restrict p = path + li * numNodes;\n        const unsigned int distIK = d[k];\n        // Branch-free form so the compiler can vectorize the inner loop.\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n            const unsigned int distIJ = d[j];\n            const unsigned int newDist = distIK + rowK[j];\n            const bool better = newDist < distIJ;\n            d[j] = better ? newDist : distIJ;\n            p[j] = better ? kk : p[j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const RowDist& rd) {\n    const size_t numNodes = rd.n;\n    if (numNodes == 0) return;\n\n    const size_t lStart = rd.localStart();\n    const size_t lCount = rd.localCount();\n\n    // Double-buffered pivot rows for the pipelined broadcast.\n    std::vector<unsigned int> rowBuf[2];\n    rowBuf[0].resize(numNodes);\n    rowBuf[1].resize(numNodes);\n\n    auto localIndexOf = [&](const size_t k) -> size_t { return k - lStart; };\n    auto isLocal = [&](const size_t k) -> bool { return k >= lStart && k < lStart + lCount; };\n\n    // Pivot row for k = 0.\n    {\n        const int own = rd.owner(0);\n        if (own == rd.rank) {\n            std::memcpy(rowBuf[0].data(), dist.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(rowBuf[0].data(), static_cast<int>(numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED, own, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n\n    MPI_Request nextReq = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n        const int cur = static_cast<int>(k & 1);\n        const int nxt = cur ^ 1;\n        const unsigned int* rowK = rowBuf[cur].data();\n\n        const bool hasNext = (k + 1 < numNodes);\n        const int nextOwner = hasNext ? rd.owner(k + 1) : -1;\n\n        size_t skipBegin = 0, skipEnd = 0;\n\n        if (hasNext) {\n            if (nextOwner == rd.rank) {\n                // Owner of row k+1: relax it first so it can be broadcast while\n                // the remaining local rows are being processed.\n                const size_t li = localIndexOf(k + 1);\n                relaxRows(dist.data(), path.data(), rowK, numNodes, k, li, li + 1);\n                std::memcpy(rowBuf[nxt].data(), dist.data() + li * numNodes,\n                            numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n                skipBegin = li;\n                skipEnd = li + 1;\n            }\n            MPI_Ibcast(rowBuf[nxt].data(), static_cast<int>(numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED, nextOwner,\n                       MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nextReq);\n        }\n\n        // Relax all remaining local rows with pivot k.\n        relaxRows(dist.data(), path.data(), rowK, numNodes, k, 0, skipBegin);\n        relaxRows(dist.data(), path.data(), rowK, numNodes, k, skipEnd, lCount);\n\n        if (hasNext) {\n            MPI_Wait(&nextReq, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        }\n    }\n    (void)isLocal;\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n    \n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n                \n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\", \n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Gather the row-distributed matrix into a full matrix on rank 0.\nstatic void gatherMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local, std::vector<unsigned int>& full,\n                         const RowDist& rd) {\n    std::vector<int> counts(rd.size), displs(rd.size);\n    for (int r = 0; r < rd.size; ++r) {\n        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rd.rowCount(r) * rd.n);\n        displs[r] = static_cast<int>(rd.rowStart(r) * rd.n);\n    }\n    if (rd.rank == 0) full.resize(rd.n * rd.n);\n    MPI_Gatherv(local.data(), static_cast<int>(rd.localCount() * rd.n), MPI_UNSIGNED,\n                rd.rank == 0 ? full.data() : nullptr, counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_UNSIGNED,\n                0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    RowDist rd;\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rd.size);\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rd.rank);\n    const bool isRoot = (rd.rank == 0);\n\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments (identical on all ranks)\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (isRoot) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (isRoot) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    rd.n = numNodes;\n    \n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", rd.size);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n    \n    // Allocate local row blocks\n    const size_t localElems = rd.localCount() * numNodes;\n    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(localElems);\n    std::vector<unsigned int> path(localElems);\n    \n    // Initialize\n    if (isRoot) printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");\n    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, rd, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);\n    initializePathMatrix(path, rd);\n    \n    // Run Floyd-Warshall\n    if (isRoot) printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    const double tStart = MPI_Wtime();\n    \n    floydWarshall(dist, path, rd);\n    \n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    const double tEnd = MPI_Wtime();\n    const long durationMs = static_cast<long>((tEnd - tStart) * 1000.0);\n    \n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", durationMs);\n        \n        // Calculate operations per second\n        // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity\n        double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;\n        double gflops = ops / (durationMs / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    }\n    \n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        std::vector<unsigned int> fullDist;\n        gatherMatrix(dist, fullDist, rd);\n\n        // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)\n        if (printResults && isRoot) {\n            print_results_int(fullDist, \"DistanceMatrix\");\n        }\n        \n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            int valid = 1;\n            if (isRoot) {\n                printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n                valid = validateResult(fullDist, numNodes) ? 1 : 0;\n                if (valid) {\n                    printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n                } else {\n                    printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                }\n            }\n            MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n            exitCode = valid ? 0 : 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\nEOF\ncat > floydwarshall/CMakeLists.txt <<'EOF'\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011CfvwdyFE98SemjKoseZtd","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01PLwLjo1qagVFrYhiC51EcE","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd floydwarshall && python3 - <<'EOF'\ns=open('floydwarshall.cpp').read()\nstart=s.index(\"// Update local rows with intermediate node k\")\nend=s.index(\"bool validateResult\")\nnew = r'''// Apply the pivots k in [k0, k1) to one local row (dist/path row of node i).\n// The pivot rows are stored contiguously in tile (row k at tile + (k-k0)*n)\n// and already carry all updates for pivots < k. Processing the pivots in\n// increasing order on the whole row reproduces exactly the sequence of\n// min-updates the classic algorithm performs on each element.\nstatic inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* __restrict d, unsigned int* __restrict p,\n                            const unsigned int* __restrict tile, const size_t numNodes,\n                            const size_t k0, const size_t k1) {\n    for (size_t k = k0; k < k1; ++k) {\n        const unsigned int* __restrict rowK = tile + (k - k0) * numNodes;\n        const unsigned int distIK = d[k];\n        const unsigned int kk = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);\n        // Branch-free form so the compiler can vectorize the inner loop.\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n            const unsigned int distIJ = d[j];\n            const unsigned int newDist = distIK + rowK[j];\n            const bool better = newDist < distIJ;\n            d[j] = better ? newDist : distIJ;\n            p[j] = better ? kk : p[j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Apply pivots [k0, k1) to local rows [lBegin, lEnd).\nstatic inline void relaxRows(unsigned int* __restrict dist, unsigned int* __restrict path,\n                             const unsigned int* __restrict tile, const size_t numNodes,\n                             const size_t k0, const size_t k1, const size_t lBegin,\n                             const size_t lEnd) {\n    for (size_t li = lBegin; li < lEnd; ++li) {\n        relaxRow(dist + li * numNodes, path + li * numNodes, tile, numNodes, k0, k1);\n    }\n}\n\n// Pivot rows are broadcast in tiles of up to TILE_ROWS consecutive rows that\n// belong to a single rank. Each local row is then updated with all pivots of\n// the tile while it stays in cache, which cuts memory traffic by ~TILE_ROWS\n// and amortizes the broadcast latency.\nconstexpr size_t TILE_ROWS = 32;\n\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const RowDist& rd) {\n    const size_t numNodes = rd.n;\n    if (numNodes == 0) return;\n\n    const size_t lStart = rd.localStart();\n    const size_t lCount = rd.localCount();\n\n    // Tile t covers pivots [k0, k1) with k1 = min(k0 + TILE_ROWS, end of owner's rows).\n    auto tileEnd = [&](const size_t k0) -> size_t {\n        const int own = rd.owner(k0);\n        return std::min({k0 + TILE_ROWS, rd.rowStart(own) + rd.rowCount(own), numNodes});\n    };\n\n    // Receive buffers (double-buffered) for tiles owned by other ranks. The\n    // owner broadcasts directly from its own dist rows (they are contiguous).\n    std::vector<unsigned int> tileBuf[2];\n    tileBuf[0].resize(TILE_ROWS * numNodes);\n    tileBuf[1].resize(TILE_ROWS * numNodes);\n\n    unsigned int* distP = dist.data();\n    unsigned int* pathP = path.data();\n\n    // Owner-side preparation of a tile: run the intra-tile pivots on the tile\n    // rows (they already carry all pivots before the tile).\n    auto prepareTile = [&](const size_t k0, const size_t k1) {\n        const size_t lt = k0 - lStart;\n        const unsigned int* tile = distP + lt * numNodes;\n        for (size_t k = k0; k < k1; ++k) {\n            for (size_t li = lt; li < lt + (k1 - k0); ++li) {\n                relaxRow(distP + li * numNodes, pathP + li * numNodes, tile, numNodes, k, k + 1);\n            }\n        }\n    };\n\n    auto tilePtr = [&](const size_t k0, const int own, const int buf) -> const unsigned int* {\n        return own == rd.rank ? distP + (k0 - lStart) * numNodes : tileBuf[buf].data();\n    };\n\n    // First tile.\n    size_t k0 = 0;\n    size_t k1 = tileEnd(0);\n    int owner = rd.owner(0);\n    int buf = 0;\n    if (owner == rd.rank) prepareTile(k0, k1);\n    MPI_Bcast(const_cast<unsigned int*>(tilePtr(k0, owner, buf)),\n              static_cast<int>((k1 - k0) * numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    MPI_Request nextReq = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;\n\n    while (k0 < numNodes) {\n        const unsigned int* tile = tilePtr(k0, owner, buf);\n\n        const bool hasNext = (k1 < numNodes);\n        const size_t n0 = k1;\n        const size_t n1 = hasNext ? tileEnd(n0) : n0;\n        const int nextOwner = hasNext ? rd.owner(n0) : -1;\n        const int nextBuf = buf ^ 1;\n\n        // Local rows that must not be touched by the generic pass below:\n        // the current tile rows (owner: already final for this tile) and the\n        // next tile rows (owner: handled first, see below).\n        size_t skipA0 = 0, skipA1 = 0, skipB0 = 0, skipB1 = 0;\n        if (owner == rd.rank) {\n            skipA0 = k0 - lStart;\n            skipA1 = k1 - lStart;\n        }\n\n        if (hasNext) {\n            if (nextOwner == rd.rank) {\n                // Owner of the next tile: bring its rows up to date with the\n                // current tile, run the intra-tile pivots, then start the\n                // broadcast so it overlaps with the remaining local work.\n                skipB0 = n0 - lStart;\n                skipB1 = n1 - lStart;\n                relaxRows(distP, pathP, tile, numNodes, k0, k1, skipB0, skipB1);\n                prepareTile(n0, n1);\n            }\n            MPI_Ibcast(const_cast<unsigned int*>(tilePtr(n0, nextOwner, nextBuf)),\n                       static_cast<int>((n1 - n0) * numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED, nextOwner,\n                       MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nextReq);\n        }\n\n        // Generic pass over all other local rows.\n        // Ranges to skip are [skipA0, skipA1) and [skipB0, skipB1); A < B.\n        if (skipA0 > skipB0) {\n            std::swap(skipA0, skipB0);\n            std::swap(skipA1, skipB1);\n        }\n        relaxRows(distP, pathP, tile, numNodes, k0, k1, 0, skipA0);\n        relaxRows(distP, pathP, tile, numNodes, k0, k1, skipA1, skipB0);\n        relaxRows(distP, pathP, tile, numNodes, k0, k1, skipB1, lCount);\n\n        if (hasNext) {\n            MPI_Wait(&nextReq, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        }\n\n        k0 = n0;\n        k1 = n1;\n        owner = nextOwner;\n        buf = nextBuf;\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns = s[:start] + new + s[end:]\nopen('floydwarshall.cpp','w').write(s)\nEOF\ncd /tmp/fwb && make 2>&1 | grep -E \"warning|error|Built\"; for n in 1 7 100 512 1000; do echo -n \"n=$n ref: \"; /tmp/fwref/ref -n $n | grep Hash; for p in 1 3 8 13; do echo -n \"  p=$p: \"; mpirun --oversubscribe -np $p ./floydwarshall -n $n -r -v | grep -E \"Hash|Validation:\" | tr '\\n' ' '; echo; done; done","description":"Replace kernel with tiled pipelined version, rebuild and verify hashes","timeout":600000},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":32,"cache_creation_input_tokens":863,"cache_read_input_tokens":38978,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":863},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7aaa6d3b-78b0-4ee3-b72a-bcde8e549973","uuid":"34ed9410-2470-40cf-9c9c-932432c91fab","timestamp":"2026-10-11T18:44:51.377Z","request_id":"req_011Cfvwdxb2xXk14QrDsVKti"}

Processing the pivots in\n// increasing order on each element reproduces exactly the sequence of\n// min-updates the classic algorithm performs, so results are bitwise equal.\n//\n// Several pivots are fused into one sweep over the row: the element d[j]\n// is loaded once, relaxed with pivots k, k+1, ... in order and stored once.\n// The required values dist[i][k+u] (after pivots < k+u) are computed up\n// front with the same chain of updates the sweep would apply to them.\ntemplate <size_t U>\nstatic inline void relaxRowFused(unsigned int* __restrict d, unsigned int* __restrict p,\n                                 const unsigned int* __restrict tile, const size_t numNodes,\n                                 const size_t k) {\n    unsigned int dik[U];\n    unsigned int kk[U];\n    for (size_t u = 0; u < U; ++u) {\n        unsigned int v = d[k + u];\n        for (size_t w = 0; w < u; ++w) {\n            const unsigned int cand = dik[w] + tile[w * numNodes + (k + u)];\n            v = cand < v ? cand : v;\n        }\n        dik[u] = v;\n        kk[u] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k + u);\n    }\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n        unsigned int distIJ = d[j];\n        unsigned int pathIJ = p[j];\n        for (size_t u = 0; u < U; ++u) {\n            const unsigned int newDist = dik[u] + tile[u * numNodes + j];\n            const bool better = newDist < distIJ;\n            distIJ = better ? newDist : distIJ;\n            pathIJ = better ? kk[u] : pathIJ;\n        }\n        d[j] = distIJ;\n        p[j] = pathIJ;\n    }\n}\n\nstatic inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* __restrict d, unsigned int* __restrict p,\n                            const unsigned int* __restrict tile, const size_t numNodes,\n                            const size_t k0, const size_t k1) {\n    size_t k = k0;\n    for (; k + 4 <= k1; k += 4) {\n        relaxRowFused<4>(d, p, tile + (k - k0) * numNodes, numNodes, k);\n    }\n    for (; k + 2 <= k1; k += 2) {\n        relaxRowFused<2>(d, p, tile + (k - k0) * numNodes, numNodes, k);\n    }\n    for (; k < k1; ++k) {\n        relaxRowFused<1>(d, p, tile + (k - k0) * numNodes, numNodes, k);\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s[:start]+new+s[end:]\nopen('floydwarshall.cpp','w').write(s)\nEOF\ncd /tmp/fwb && make 2>&1 | grep -E \"warning|error|Built\"; for n in 7 100 513 1000; do echo -n \"n=$n ref: \"; /tmp/fwref/ref -n $n | grep Hash; for p in 1 3 13; do echo -n \"  p=$p: \"; mpirun --oversubscribe -np $p ./floydwarshall -n $n -r -v 2>/dev/null | grep -E \"Hash|Validation:\" | tr '\\n' ' '; echo; done; done; echo -n \"n=2048 p=1: \"; mpirun -np 1 ./floydwarshall -n 2048 -r | grep -E \"Hash|time\" | tr '\\n' ' '; echo","description":"Fuse four pivots per sweep, rebuild, verify hashes and single-rank timing","timeout":600000},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":32,"cache_creation_input_tokens":799,"cache_read_input_tokens":54765,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":799},"output_tokens":7,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7aaa6d3b-78b0-4ee3-b72a-bcde8e549973","uuid":"c57a6b9b-a320-4baf-a81f-929e4dc9c76e","timestamp":"2026-10-11T18:50:46.750Z","request_id":"req_011CfvxALMK79zEZCdN7nWeZ"}

If req is non-null,\n// the pending broadcast is polled between rows so that MPI makes progress on\n// it while we compute (Open MPI only progresses in MPI calls).\nstatic inline void relaxRows(unsigned int* __restrict dist, unsigned int* __restrict path,\n                             const unsigned int* __restrict tile, const size_t numNodes,\n                             const size_t k0, const size_t k1, const size_t lBegin,\n                             const size_t lEnd, MPI_Request* req) {\n    for (size_t li = lBegin; li < lEnd; ++li) {\n        relaxRow(dist + li * numNodes, path + li * numNodes, tile, numNodes, k0, k1);\n        if (req != nullptr && *req != MPI_REQUEST_NULL) {\n            int flag = 0;\n            MPI_Test(req, &flag, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Pivot rows are broadcast in tiles of up to TILE_ROWS consecutive rows that\n// belong to a single rank. Each local row is then updated with all pivots of\n// the tile while it stays in cache, which cuts memory traffic by ~TILE_ROWS\n// and amortizes the broadcast latency.\nconstexpr size_t TILE_ROWS = 32;\n\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const RowDist& rd) {\n    const size_t numNodes = rd.n;\n    if (numNodes == 0) return;\n\n    const size_t lStart = rd.localStart();\n    const size_t lCount = rd.localCount();\n\n    // Tile covers pivots [k0, k1) with k1 = min(k0 + TILE_ROWS, end of owner's rows).\n    auto tileEnd = [&](const size_t k0) -> size_t {\n        const int own = rd.owner(k0);\n        return std::min({k0 + TILE_ROWS, rd.rowStart(own) + rd.rowCount(own), numNodes});\n    };\n\n    // Double-buffered tile storage. Tile row (k - k0) holds row k in the state\n    // \"all pivots < k applied\" (the state the classic algorithm reads it in).\n    std::vector<unsigned int> tileBuf[2];\n    tileBuf[0].resize(TILE_ROWS * numNodes);\n    tileBuf[1].resize(TILE_ROWS * numNodes);\n\n    unsigned int* distP = dist.data();\n    unsigned int* pathP = path.data();\n\n    // Owner-side preparation of a tile [k0, k1) into buf: for k = k0, k0+1,\n    // ... relax row k with the tile pivots k0..k-1 (fused, using the\n    // snapshots already in buf) and snapshot it. The remaining pivots\n    // k+1..k1-1 for these rows are applied later in the regular pass.\n    auto prepareTile = [&](const size_t k0, const size_t k1, unsigned int* buf) {\n        for (size_t k = k0; k < k1; ++k) {\n            const size_t lk = k - lStart;\n            unsigned int* row = distP + lk * numNodes;\n            relaxRow(row, pathP + lk * numNodes, buf, numNodes, k0, k);\n            std::memcpy(buf + (k - k0) * numNodes, row, numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        }\n    };\n\n    // Finish the owner's own tile rows: row k still needs pivots k+1..k1-1\n    // (pivot k itself never changes row k because dist[k][k] == 0).\n    auto finishTileRows = [&](const size_t k0, const size_t k1, const unsigned int* buf,\n                              MPI_Request* req) {\n        for (size_t k = k0; k < k1; ++k) {\n            const size_t lk = k - lStart;\n            relaxRow(distP + lk * numNodes, pathP + lk * numNodes, buf, numNodes, k + 1, k1);\n            if (req != nullptr && *req != MPI_REQUEST_NULL) {\n                int flag = 0;\n                MPI_Test(req, &flag, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n            }\n        }\n    };\n\n    // First tile.\n    size_t k0 = 0;\n    size_t k1 = tileEnd(0);\n    int owner = rd.owner(0);\n    int buf = 0;\n    if (owner == rd.rank) prepareTile(k0, k1, tileBuf[buf].data());\n    MPI_Bcast(tileBuf[buf].data(), static_cast<int>((k1 - k0) * numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED, owner,\n              MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    MPI_Request nextReq = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;\n\n    while (k0 < numNodes) {\n        const unsigned int* tile = tileBuf[buf].data();\n\n        const bool hasNext = (k1 < numNodes);\n        const size_t n0 = k1;\n        const size_t n1 = hasNext ? tileEnd(n0) : n0;\n        const int nextOwner = hasNext ? rd.owner(n0) : -1;\n        const int nextBuf = buf ^ 1;\n\n        // Local row ranges that get special treatment:\n        //   [skipA0, skipA1): rows of the current tile (owner only)\n        //   [skipB0, skipB1): rows of the next tile (next owner only)\n        size_t skipA0 = 0, skipA1 = 0, skipB0 = 0, skipB1 = 0;\n        if (owner == rd.rank) {\n            skipA0 = k0 - lStart;\n            skipA1 = k1 - lStart;\n        }\n\n        if (hasNext) {\n            if (nextOwner == rd.rank) {\n                // Owner of the next tile: bring its rows up to date with the\n                // current tile, prepare the tile, then start the broadcast so\n                // it overlaps with the remaining local work.\n                skipB0 = n0 - lStart;\n                skipB1 = n1 - lStart;\n                relaxRows(distP, pathP, tile, numNodes, k0, k1, skipB0, skipB1, nullptr);\n                prepareTile(n0, n1, tileBuf[nextBuf].data());\n            }\n            MPI_Ibcast(tileBuf[nextBuf].data(), static_cast<int>((n1 - n0) * numNodes),\n                       MPI_UNSIGNED, nextOwner, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nextReq);\n        }\n\n        // Regular pass over all other local rows (ranges A and B excluded).\n        if (skipA0 > skipB0) {\n            std::swap(skipA0, skipB0);\n            std::swap(skipA1, skipB1);\n        }\n        relaxRows(distP, pathP, tile, numNodes, k0, k1, 0, skipA0, &nextReq);\n        relaxRows(distP, pathP, tile, numNodes, k0, k1, skipA1, skipB0, &nextReq);\n        relaxRows(distP, pathP, tile, numNodes, k0, k1, skipB1, lCount, &nextReq);\n\n        // Owner of the current tile: complete its own tile rows.\n        if (owner == rd.rank) {\n            finishTileRows(k0, k1, tile, &nextReq);\n        }\n\n        if {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011CfvygooX5NpXzJCPNRTv2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The work is complete. The benchmark in `floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp` is now a pure MPI program, and `CMakeLists.txt` links it against MPI with `find_package(MPI REQUIRED)`. No new files were created and the executable name is unchanged.\n\n**How it is parallelized**\n\n- **Row distribution.** The kernel addresses the matrix with row i contiguous in memory, so each rank owns a contiguous block of rows of both the distance and path matrices. Nothing else is replicated.\n- **Tiled pivot broadcast.** Pivot rows are broadcast in tiles of 8 to 32 consecutive rows (chosen from rows per rank). Each local row is updated with the whole tile while it sits in cache, which removed the memory-bandwidth cliff I saw when a rank's block outgrew L3.\n- **Pipelined communication.** The owner of the next tile prepares it first and starts a non-blocking broadcast before processing its remaining rows. Other ranks poll the request between rows so Open MPI actually progresses it during compute.\n- **Fused, vectorized inner loop.** Four pivots are applied per sweep over a row with a branch-free min/blend, so each element is loaded and stored once per four pivots. GCC vectorizes it with AVX2 and emits no masked stores.\n- **Initialization and output.** Every rank replays the same seeded rand_r stream and keeps only its rows, so no scatter is needed. The full matrix is gathered to rank 0 only when -r or -v is given. Timing uses MPI_Wtime between barriers. Rank 0 does all printing, and the validation result is broadcast so every rank returns the same exit code.\n\n**Exactness.** Each element receives the same sequence of strict-less-than min updates in the same pivot order as the original, so both the distance and path matrices are bitwise identical. I verified the result hash against a copy of the original program for n in {1, 7, 100, 513, 1000, 2048, 4096, 8192} and rank counts from 1 to 130, including more ranks than rows. All matched and validation passed.\n\n**Performance** on this 2-socket EPYC 7763 node (core-bound runs):\n\n| n | original (1 core) | 1 rank | 16 ranks | 64 ranks | 128 ranks |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 2048 | 13.9 s | 1.36 s | 0.11 s (13 ranks) | 0.065 s | |\n| 4096 | | 10.2 s | 0.71 s | 0.24 s | 0.27 s |\n| 8192 | | | | 1.61 s | 1.14 s |\n\nThe single-rank version is about ten times faster than the original due to tiling and fusion. Scaling flattens past 64 ranks at n=4096 because each rank then holds only 32 rows and the per-tile broadcast plus the owner's serial tile preparation dominate. Larger problems keep scaling, as the 8192 row shows.\n\n**Known limits.** Divisions by zero in the printed GOPS figure when the time rounds to 0 ms are preserved from the original. Build artifacts and the reference copy used for checking live only under /tmp."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":32,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1668,"cache_read_input_tokens":78208,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1668},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7aaa6d3b-78b0-4ee3-b72a-bcde8e549973","uuid":"c6c38539-38a5-4178-a2e7-0c89fe7120e8","timestamp":"2026-10-11T19:10:32.528Z","request_id":"req_011CfvygoTBTL72KLazqQXve"}
